## The frozen findings

- **FPR32-M17CD-01 (H): the claimed material-consumption correction is still not a real executor binding.** The production workflow selected by `workflows/manifest.py` is `hunyuan_i2v_v1`, whose node `31` is an ordinary KSampler; the test helper `_performance_manifest()` manufactures `controls_file`/`audio_file`/`fps_*` fields on that KSampler and the corrected translator writes uploaded filenames into those synthetic fields. NO LivePortrait/performance workflow package exists in the corrected tree or delta — the frozen source-fit contract requires the derived bytes to bind into the PINNED LivePortrait package, not arbitrary undeclared fields on a KSampler. FPR31-02 not closed; the corrected G06 cannot become a valid proof merely by installing the external stack.
- **FPR32-M17CD-02 (H): lawful multi-segment performance cannot pass the graph binder.** The frozen GPI convention deliberately creates repeated grouped keys (two segments → two `performance.controls` rows by position), but `_bind_schema5_derived()` keys `supplied` by `input_key` alone and raises "duplicate performance derived input" on the second segment; the fixture manifest compounds it with cardinality 1. The create path proves a lawful two-segment Generation whose worker submission is therefore deterministically refused; the production-path test and G06 are single-segment so the green CI cannot see it.
- **FPR32-M17CD-03 (M): the retained-manifest-schema-3 lower-logical path is broken for schema 5.** `_drive` computes `logical_schema` via the projection but then passes `logical_schema_version=spec["schema_version"]` (=5) to `project_lower_logical_execution_view` (accepts only 1 or 2); the v2 closure tests `spec["schema_version"] == 2` (false for v5). The terminal output resolution has the mirror defect (the retained-schema-3 branch excludes 5; the schema-5 branch parses the schema-3 manifest as v1/v2). The new test checks only a synthetic helper, never the real branches.
- **FPR32-M17CD-04 (M): `expected_lower_from_retained()` does not reconstruct exact lower-v2 meaning.** It always returns a schema-1 document; worker/recovery strip `schema_version`/`model`/`realization` before equality with only model id/version separately compared; the inherited schema-2 worker law verifies model/profile/fingerprint but does not reconstruct the complete captured `realization` object, and recovery runs no full v2 historical validation — a coherent mutation of an unconsumed realization coordinate (e.g. `parameter_overrides`) is outside the claimed independent equality proof.
- **FPR32-M17CD-05 (M): two frozen vocal coordinates ungrounded in the v5 mirror.** `captured_children` validation compares VP id/binding hash/interval/rate but never independently validates `vocal_audio_blob_hash` (shape-only) or `synchronization_basis_version` (unconstrained) against the retained VP/binding authority; a coherently rehashed spec can alter them undetected.
- **FPR32-M17CD-06 (M): the schema-5/v1 worker does not authenticate stored WorkflowSpec bytes against `workflow_spec_hash` before submission** (the schema-2/3/4 paths do; recovery does). The divergence makes the ungrounded-vocal tamper easier to reach.

# M17C-D thirty-fourth fresh independent first-pass review — 2026-10-08 — NOT CLEAN (FPR34-M17CD-01..06 frozen)

Green CI is valid corroboration and does not close the findings.**

## The frozen findings

- **FPR34-M17CD-01 (H): the LivePortrait execution graph is still not executable or materially consuming the controls.** `LivePortraitApply.apply()` validates the controls then `return (image,)` — no schedule applied, audio and rasterization unused; and the graph's node-31 KSampler connections violate the stock contract (`model` receives an IMAGE output; `positive`/`negative` receive STRING outputs; `latent_image` cites `[50, 1]` where node 50 declares one output; the manifest's video output names node 31's `images` field but a sampler is not a media-output node and no SaveAnimatedWEBP-equivalent exists). Required: a genuine executor-native application edge + type-correct connections + a real video-output path; non-live tests must verify the COMPLETE graph's node signatures, connection types, output indexes, and the output-producing terminal node — not class-name presence.
- **FPR34-M17CD-02 (H): the performance attestation exists but is NOT enforced by the schema-5 worker before submission.** `check_performance_runtime()` is never invoked by `_drive`; the v5-over-v1 path performs ordinary schema-1 preparation with NO performance attestation; the inherited v2 path loads the GGUF-only default whitelist (a performance-whitelisted deployment is not universally accepted there); the retained-3 path inherits yet another custom-node identity. Required: ONE effective performance-runtime check at the schema-5 execution boundary binding the captured package requirements to the attested process + implementation hash, for BOTH lower forms, without weakening the predecessor contracts; the negative test proves a valid-package schema-5 Generation with a GGUF-only / missing / wrong-hash performance attestation refuses BEFORE submission.
- **FPR34-M17CD-03 (H): a lawful generic (no-vocal) Generation deterministically fails at the executor edge.** The frozen law permits `vocal: null`; the worker correctly omits the vocal-audio bundle; the manifest declares the input optional — but the graph PERMANENTLY connects node 41 whose `load()` unconditionally `_load_bundle("")`. Required: a lawful no-vocal execution representation at the executor edge (no invented GPI row, no change to the vocal-iff-dialogue law) + a genuine generic-only production submission and executor-node regression.
- **FPR34-M17CD-04 (H): the retained lower-v2 reconstruction reads MUTABLE current VisualFacet requirements.** The recompilation queries `visual_facets.requirement`, but `PATCH /visual-facets/{id}` lawfully flips required↔optional — a post-capture reclassification changes the compiler's readiness outcome, so a valid captured v5-over-v2 Generation can become non-executable and staged recovery can reject its historical realization on a lawful current-state edit. Required: recover the facet requirement that belonged to the CAPTURED authority; the decisive test creates a lawful v2 Generation, mutates the requirement through the public API, and proves worker + recovery still certify the originally captured realization.
- **FPR34-M17CD-05 (M): retained profile/fingerprint selection is not uniquely anchored to the captured release.** The workflow-id-scan uniqueness assumption breaks when two lawful releases of one workflow coexist in the store; the fingerprint model-id match can depend on scan order. Required: resolve identity through a demonstrable captured-release relationship + content-addressed bytes; the regression retains TWO lawful releases of one workflow and proves both older and newer Generations reconstruct against their own packages in worker and recovery.
- **FPR34-M17CD-06 (M, proof gap): the retained-3 production tests abort AT submission — the terminal output-resolution path (submitted/observed lifecycle, history retrieval, the retained-3 output-manifest projection, resolve_comfy_outputs, import) is never traversed despite the claim.** Required: a non-live production-path test completing the submission protocol with a recorded prompt id + deterministic terminal history fixtures, traversing the ACTUAL retained-3 output-resolution branch for both v5-over-v1 and v5-over-v2.
